Potential better optimizer based on exact active + line search:
```
def active_gradient(c, a, top=1, soft_temp=0.0):
    if top>=m or soft_temp...:
       # weights
    vmax = c.max()
    if hard top:
       idx = np.argpartition(c, -top)[-top:]
       w = np.zeros(m); w[idx] = 1; w/=top
    else:
       z = (c - vmax)/temp_scale? # values ~1/n; use scale = n? Better v = c * (score/(2n))? Use v = n*c.
       w = softmax(v/temp)
    Frev = rfft(a[::-1], L)
    W = rfft(w, L)
    g = irfft(W*Frev, L)[(n-1):m]
    g=max(g,0)
    # derivative factor 2 already maybe.
    return g
```

```
 start=time
 budget=budget_s
 n=choose length
 L,next pow

 best_a=parent; best_score=score
 candidate pool = [parent]

 full_from_coarse = upsample(best_coarse)
 polish full for 30s, add
 # main loop alternating:
   - For each candidate from pool (top few), run full_polish time chunk (30-120s) depending mode (soft/hard/lp direction)
   - Periodically generate perturbations (noisy, harsh delete, reverse, coarse LP direction) and add to pool
   - Update best

 return best by exact.
```

```
 score=2n*max(c)
 best...
 temp=t0 # relative (cmax-scale)
 hard=hard0
 eta=step0
 staleness=0
 iter=0
 while time<deadline:
    c = conv_self(a)
    cmax = c.max()
    score = 2n*cmax
    if score < best: update
    if best_score <= 1.001? maybe stop? but lower bound ~1. We can stop local if no improvement.
    # choose top K: K = max(5, int(m*0.002) + something?) For full m=12241, K=64; coarse m=255, K=20.

    # soft weights temp temp_rel

    z=clip(-50,0)
    w_soft = exp(z); normalize; if not finite uniform.
    w = hard*w_hard + (1-hard)*w_soft
    # gradient
    g = cross_corr(w,a)
    gmax = g.max(); if zero: break
    gnorm = g/gmax
    # accept steps maybe eta list: eta, 0.5eta, 0.75eta, 1.5eta? To explore, try increasing occasionally.
    for step in (eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta, 1.5*eta, 0.75*eta):

       a2=norm
```

```
 start=time.monotonic()
 budget=...
 total = max(5, budget)
 # choose n
 n = choose length
 L = next_pow2(2n-1)
 m=2n-1
 rng = np.random.default_rng(12345+seed)

 parent = get_parent(n)? If parent length not n, if n==parent len use; else interpolate.
 if parent is None: parent = uniform.

 # helper exact score
 best_a = normalize(parent); best_score = internal_score
 pool = [(best_a,best_score)]

 # time allocations:
 t_reserve_eval = min(120, 0.05*total)
 t_coarse = 0.0
 if total > 600: t_coarse = min(300, 0.12*total)
 t_first = min(400, 0.2*total) if total large else 0.2*total
 t_final = min(500, 0.25*total)
 main_time = total - t_reserve_eval - t_coarse - t_first - t_final; ensure positive.

 # 1. coarse if time:

    coarse_best = coarse_search(n, best_a, rng, t_coarse)
    if coarse_best is not None:
       full_from_coarse = upsample(coarse_best, best_a, n)
       sc = score(full_from_coarse); add_pool
       # polish full_from_coarse for min(180, 0.12*total) within main? maybe dedicated.

 # 2. polish best parent (or current best) for t_first:

 update.

 # 3. polish recent best_coarse candidate for t_coarse_full = min(300,0.15*total):

    tmp, sc = _optimize_sequence(coarse_candidate, ...)

 # 4. Main loop until hard deadline:
 candidates to explore = top pool + random perturbations
 cycle_no=0
 while time < main_deadline:
   rem = main_deadline - now
   chunk = min(120, max(20, 0.15*rem)) # 90s
   mode=cycle%4
   base = best_a (or pool random)
   if mode==0: perturb = noise_mix(best_a, alpha=0.3, rng)
   elif mode==1: perturb = harsh_delete(best_a, rng)

   else: perturb = random_prior or random prior mix

   update pool/best
   cycle+=1
   # if no improvement for several cycles, increase perturb strength

 # 5. Final polish best for t_final:

 update.

 # 7. build list of candidates: best, top pool; maybe scale? call external score for each if time.
 return best exact.
```

```
def _mws_direction(f, L, m, n, prior, inner=20, eta_start=0.5, eta_end=5.0):
   c = conv_self(f); M=c.max()
   if M<=0 return prior
   g = prior copy; y uniform
   avg = zeros; count=0
   for it:
       eta = ...
       # Constraint f*g <= M. Compute u = f*g (irfft F_f * F_g)
       # Excess r = max(u/M - 1, 0). Update y proportional to exp(eta*r + small_uniform)
       y = ...
       # Dual A^T y >= 1? Compute s = conv with y and f_rev? A^T y coordinate = Σ_k y_k f_{k-i} (times scale). Need constraint scaling? A_f*y >= 1. Use s.

       g = exp(-eta*s/M + logprior) normalize

```

Choosing T and weights inside `_optimize_sequence`:
```
def _choose_weights(c, temp_rel, hard_mix, m):
    cmax = c.max()
    if cmax <=0: return uniform, cmax
    K = max(8, min(256, int(m/400)))  # full 30, coarse 8
    if K < m:
       idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       min_top = c[idx].min()
    else idx all
    spread = cmax - min_top
    T = max(cmax*0.005, spread*0.25, 1e-15)
    # Adjust by temp_rel? temp_rel as multiplier: T = max(T, temp_rel*cmax)
    T = max(T, temp_rel*cmax)
    w_soft = softmax((c-cmax)/T)
    if hard_mix >0:
       w_hard = zeros; w_hard[idx]=1.0; w_hard/=len(idx)
       w = hard_mix*w_hard + (1-hard_mix)*w_soft
    else w=w_soft
    return w/c? normalized
```

Let's implement `_balanced_active_weights(c, p, n, max_lag=0, max_coord=0)`:
```
def _balanced_weights(c, p, n, n_lags=64, n_coords=128):
    m=len(c)
    K = min(m, max(8, n_lags))
    S = min(n, max(16, n_coords))
    idxk = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:] if K<m else np.arange(m)
    idxs = np.argpartition(p, -S)[-S:] if S<n else np.arange(n)
    H = np.zeros((S,K))
    p_local = p
    for j,k in enumerate(idxk):
        q = k - idxs
        mask = (q>=0)&(q<n)
        H[mask, j] = p_local[q[mask]]
    # solve H λ ≈ 1_nonneg
    try lsq_linear
    else lstsq
    lam = max(λ,0); s=lam.sum(); if s>0 lam/=s else uniform over idxk
    return lam, idxk
```
We need pass only active set coefficients, not full length? For gradient, we can build full weight `w` zeros and set w[idxk]=lam. Or return full w. To blend, full w.

Lint: `q = k - idxs` int; mask boolean; q[mask] valid. Good. If K and S 64/128, H entries small; target 1. If solution huge, normalized. If `lstsq` returns nan, fallback.

```
   rem=deadline-now
   if rem < 0.5: break
   c = conv_self(a); cmax=...
   score = 2*n*cmax
   if score < best_score - 1e-13: best...; no_accep=0
   if best_score <= 1.0005: break? lower bound but target>1. If score < 1.001 maybe stop. But no.
   # time frac
   frac = (now-start)/max(time_limit - 1e-9,1e-9)
   bal_nom = bal_start + (bal_end-bal_start)*min(1, frac)
   if no_accep > 40: bal_eff = 0.15; T_mult = max(temp,0.5)
   else: bal_eff = bal_nom
   # temp ensure >0

   if it%2==0:
       w_bal_full = _balanced_weights(c,a,n, dynamic K,S)
   else reuse
   w = (1-bal_eff)*w_soft + bal_eff*w_bal_full
   g = _cross_corr(w,a)
   gmax = g.max(); if gmax<=0: break
   gnorm=g/gmax
   cur_lse = _lse(c,T)
   accepted=False
   # step list based on eta
   steps = [eta, 0.5*eta, min(1.0, 1.5*eta)]
   # if eta small<0.02, add 0.1? optional

       if step<=1e-6: continue

       c2=conv_self

           accept
       elif lse2 < cur_lse - 1e-10 and s2 <= best_score + 1e-5:
           accept
   if accepted: ...
   else: ...
   it+=1
```

Let's design `_random_prior` robust:
```
def _random_prior(n, rng):
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=float)/(max(1,n-1))
    z = np.zeros(n)
    z += rng.normal(0,0.2)
    # low freq: up to min(8,n//2)
    for k in range(1, min(9, n//2+1)):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.15, 1.0) / math.sqrt(k)
        ph = rng.uniform(0, 2π)
        z += amp*np.cos(2π*k*t + ph)
        amp2 = rng.uniform(0.15, 1.0) / math.sqrt(k)
        ph2 = rng.uniform(0,2π)
        z += amp2*np.sin(2π*k*t + ph2)
    # one antisymmetric ramp if n>10:
    z += rng.uniform(-1,1) * (t - 0.5)
    # optional high freq smoothing noise
    noise = rng.normal(0,0.2,n)
    z += noise
    # maybe smooth noise
    p = np.exp(z - z.max())
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    return normalize(p)
```
This may produce very peaked sequences if amplitudes large; okay.

`_noise_mix` uses prior. If using log with zeros: floor = 1e-8? For p with zeros, log(0+1e-8) big negative; mixing may introduce mass at zeros. Use floor = 1e-6 * max(a) maybe. But if a has zeros we may want revive. For noise mix, use `a_safe = np.maximum(a, 1e-6*max)`; prior separately. `z = (1-α)log(a_safe)+αlog(prior_safe)`. Normalize. Good.

`_harsh_mix`:
```
def _harsh_mix(a, rng):
   keep = rng.uniform(0.4,0.75)
   # deterministic top + random
   thresh = np.quantile(a, 1-keep)
   mask = (a >= thresh)
   # Also random mask with same keep to allow new positions
   rand = (rng.random(n) < keep)
   mask = mask | rand
   a2 = a * mask
   a2 = np.maximum(a2, 0)
   s=a2.sum(); if s<0.01: a2 = a.copy()
   return normalize(a2)
```
This may produce sparse.

```
   try: res=_LINPROG(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, 1000.0), method='highs')
   except: ...
   if res.success and res.x is not None:
      g=np.asarray(res.x,float); if finite and sum>0 return normalize(g)
   return None
```

Let's now think about possible numerical failure in `_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a):
   a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a[~np.isfinite] =0
   a[a<0]=0
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
   a = a/s
   # prevent underflow zeros? If max(a)>0, a = np.maximum(a, tiny) maybe changes sum; renormalize.
   tiny = np.finfo(np.float64).tiny
   if a.max() > 0:
       a = np.maximum(a, tiny)
       s2=a.sum()
       if s2>0: a/=s2
   return a
```
If we set tiny ~2e-308 for all zeros, sum becomes n*2e-308 underflow? Actually n*2e-308 ~1e-304, fine >0? float min normal 2e-308, subnormal tiny? `np.finfo.tiny` is min normal. Sum 6k*2e-308 ~1e-304, representable. Then normalize -> uniform? Good. But adding tiny to all zeros makes uniform; if we wanted exact zeros, this destroys. In normalization during optimization, using tiny for all zeros may reintroduce all previously zero coordinates with negligible mass, good for escape but maybe makes support dense. Tiny 2e-308 negligible relative 1e-4, won't affect. Good.

But if a has exact zeros and we multiply by exp(-step*g), a2 tiny. Sum normalization leaves tiny. So eventually all coordinates small but >0. That's okay. We don't want hard zeros. Harsh mix can create larger zeros but normalize adds tiny. Fine.

Let's write `_balanced_weights` with fallback to top active threshold:
```
function _balanced_weights(c, p, n, lag_count=0, coord_count=0):
    m=len(c)
    K = lag_count if lag_count>0 else min(m, max(8, int(m/220)))
    K = min(K,m)
    S = coord_count if coord_count>0 else min(n, max(24, int(n/50)))
    S = min(S,n)
    # active indices: threshold to avoid only top if many near max
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if K < m:
       idx_top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    else idx_top = np.arange(m)
    # threshold: c >= cmax - max(cmax*0.05, 0)
    thr = cmax - max(1e-15, cmax*0.05)
    idx_thr = np.where(c >= thr)[0]
    if 0 < len(idx_thr) < K:
       # take idx_thr plus fill top? Use idx_thr only? Could be fewer than K; if too few, top.
       idx = idx_thr
    elif len(idx_thr) > K:
       # choose top K within idx_thr
       idx = idx_top? Need top among idx_thr. Since idx_top may include some below threshold. Easier: sort idx_thr by c descending and take K. Use np.argsort(c[idx_thr])[-K:]
    else:
       idx = idx_top
    # Ensure unique? If idx_thr used maybe unsorted but fine.
    # select support S top p by quantile
    if S < n:
       idxs = np.argpartition(p, -S)[-S:]
    else: idxs = np.arange(n)
    H = np.zeros((S, len(idx)))
    for j,k in enumerate(idx):
       q = k - idxs
       valid = (q>=0) & (q<n)
       H[valid, j] = p[q[valid]]
    # solve nonnegative LS
    lam=None
    try:
       lam1, residuals, rank, s = np.linalg.lstsq(H, np.ones(S), rcond=None)
       lam = np.maximum(lam1, 0.0)
    except Exception: lam=None
    if lam is None or not finite or lam.sum() <=0:
       w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=1/len(idx); return w
    lam=lam/lam.sum()
    w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=lam
    return w
```
If idx length too small (1), LS okay; H one column solution λ=1. Fine.

Could H sol λ with huge values cause p? normalize. Good.

Potential: The equation Hλ=1 may be underdetermined if len(idx)<S; lstsq gives min norm. Fine.

Let's define `_external_score(seq)`:
```
def _external_score(seq):
   try:
      val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
   except NameError:
      return _internal_exact(seq)
   except Exception:
      return float('inf')
   if val is None: return inf
   return float(val)
```

```
def _internal_exact(seq):
   a=np.asarray(seq,float); a=np.maximum(a,0); a=np.minimum(a,1000)
   n=len(a); s=a.sum(); if s<0.01: return inf
   b=np.convolve(a,a); mb=b.max(); return float(2*n*mb/(s*s))
```

In `_optimize_sequence`, if time_limit <=0 return normalized and score. Need avoid if `L` less than m? Caller passes.

Let's code `_optimize_sequence` carefully:
```
def _optimize_sequence(a, n, L, m, rng, time_limit, temp_start=0.15, temp_end=0.02, bal_start=0.3, bal_end=0.5, step_start=0.08):
    t0=time.monotonic(); dl=t0+max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    a=_normalize(a)
    if time_limit <= 0.5:
       sc=_score_a(a,n,L,m); return a, sc
    c=_conv_self(a,L,m); score=2*n*float(np.max(c)); best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    temp=float(temp_start); eta=float(step_start); no_accep=0; it=0
    last_bal=None
    bal_lags = max(8, min(128, int(m/180)))
    bal_coords = max(24, min(256, int(n/40)))
    while time.monotonic() < dl:
       now=time.monotonic(); rem=dl-now; if rem<0.25: break
       c=_conv_self(a,L,m); cmax=float(np.max(c)); score=2*n*cmax
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=score; best=a.copy(); no_accep=0
       # if at lower bound or no time
       if best_score <= 1.0005: break
       frac=(now-t0)/max(time_limit,1e-9); frac=min(max(frac,0),1)
       bal_nom = bal_start + (bal_end-bal_start)*frac
       if no_accep > 50:
          bal_eff = min(0.2, bal_nom)
          temp_use = max(temp, 0.5)
       else: bal_eff = bal_nom; temp_use=temp
       T = max(1e-15, temp_use * cmax)
       w_soft = _soft_weights(c, T)
       if last_bal is None or (it % 2 == 0):
           last_bal = _balanced_weights(c, a, n, bal_lags, bal_coords)
       w = (1.0 - bal_eff) * w_soft + bal_eff * last_bal
       g = _cross_corr(w, a, L, m, n)
       gmax=float(np.max(g))
       if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 0: break
       gnorm = g / gmax
       cur_lse = _lse(c, T)
       steps = [eta, 0.5*eta, min(1.0, 1.5*eta)]
       # maybe add 0.1 if eta tiny
       if eta < 0.05: steps.append(0.1)
       accepted=False; chosen=0.0
       for step in steps:
          if step <= 1e-6 or step > 2.0: continue
          a2 = a * np.exp(-step * gnorm)
          a2 = _normalize(a2)
          c2 = _conv_self(a2,L,m)
          s2 = 2*n*float(np.max(c2))
          lse2 = _lse(c2, T)
          if (s2 < best_score - 1e-12) or (s2 < score - 1e-13):
              a=a2; accepted=True; chosen=step; break
          elif lse2 < cur_lse - 1e-11 and s2 <= best_score + 1e-7:
              a=a2; accepted=True; chosen=step; break
       if accepted:
          if chosen >= eta - 1e-12:
             eta = min(0.8, eta * 1.10)
          elif chosen > 0.7*eta:
             eta = min(0.8, eta * 1.02)
          else:
             eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.92)
          if best_score < score: no_accep=0
          # If improved best, sharpen temp; if only surrogate, keep
          temp = max(temp_end, temp * 0.975)
          if no_accep == 0 and it % 100 ==0:
             # occasional reheat? no
             pass
       else:
          no_accep += 1
          eta = max(0.015, eta * 0.78)
          temp = min(1.5, temp * 1.20)
          if no_accep > 90:
             # perturbation reset to best with random
             alpha = 0.25 + 0.35*float(rng.random())
             prior = _random_prior(n, rng)
             a = _normalize((1.0-alpha)*best + alpha*prior)
             c = _conv_self(a,L,m); score=2*n*float(np.max(c))
             # don't update best if worse
             no_accep = 0
             temp = max(temp_start*0.7, temp*0.5)
             eta = max(step_start*0.6, 0.05)
       it += 1
    c=_conv_self(best,L,m); best_score=2*n*float(np.max(c))
    return best, best_score
```
If `score` initial is inf? no. If `cmax` zero? no.

Note: If `a2` normalized adds tiny to zeros, gnorm maybe all finite. Good.

`temp` lower/upper: temp_start 0.15; temp_end 0.02; no_accept can push to 1.5. Relative to cmax. If temp=1.5 T large, soft weights uniform-ish. Fine.

`bal_eff` maybe >1? Ensure bal_end <=1. Use min(0.9). In calls.

Could stopping at best_score <=1.0005 be wasted; target much above. Good. But due lower bound 1.00008, if somehow near 1.0005, stop. Fine.

Potential issue: When reheat uses `best` but `best` may be deflated. Good. It doesn't update current score to new; next loop recomputes. Fine.

Now, `_mws_direction` code:
```
def _mws_direction(f, L, m, n, prior, inner, eta_start=2.0, eta_end=30.0):
   f=_normalize(f); prior=_normalize(prior)
   inner=max(1,int(inner))
   F=np.fft.rfft(f,L); Frev=np.fft.rfft(f[::-1],L)
   x=prior.copy(); y=np.full(m, 1.0/m); avg=np.zeros(n); count=0
   logprior=np.log(np.maximum(prior,1e-300))
   for it in range(inner):
      frac = 0 if inner<=1 else it/(inner-1)
      eta=eta_start + (eta_end-eta_start)*frac
      X=np.fft.rfft(x,L); u=np.fft.irfft(F*X,L)[:m]; np.maximum(u,0,out=u)
      vu=n*u; vma=float(np.max(vu))
      z=eta*(vu-vma); z=np.clip(z,-50,0); ye=np.exp(z); ys=float(np.sum(ye))
      if not finite or ys<=0: y.fill(1/m) else y=ye/ys
      avg += x; count +=1
      Y=np.fft.rfft(y,L); s=np.fft.irfft(Y*Frev,L)[n-1:n-1+n]; np.maximum(s,0,out=s)
      ps=n*s; sma=float(np.max(ps))
      zx = -eta*(ps-sma) + 0.8*logprior
      zx -= float(np.max(zx)); zx=np.clip(zx,-50,0); xe=np.exp(zx); xs=float(np.sum(xe))
      if not finite or xs<=0: x=prior.copy();
      else: x=xe/xs
   if count: return _normalize(0.5*(avg/count)+0.5*x)
   return prior
```
This may produce weights but not exact. Good.

`_mws_start(base, L,m,n, score, rng, time_chunk?)`: It doesn't need time, just inner. Use inner 12. Then line search alpha.
```
def _mws_start(base, L,m,n,rng):
   prior=_random_prior(n,rng)
   d=_mws_direction(base,L,m,n,prior,12,2.0,30.0)
   if d is None: return base
   best_a=base; best_sc=_score_a(base,n,L,m)
   for alpha in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
       a2=_normalize((1-alpha)*base+alpha*d)
       sc=_score_a(a2,n,L,m)
       if sc<best_sc: best_a=a2; best_sc=sc
   if best_sc >= _score_a(base,...): maybe return normalize(0.5base+0.5d)
   return best_a
```

```
def _coarse_search(parent, n, rng, time_limit):
   if time_limit <= 10: return None
   if parent is None: M=128? else

      sc=_score_a(arr,M,Lc,mc); add
   # runs: start with best, then mix random, then random own

   for start_arr, frac in runs:

      arr, sc = _optimize_sequence(start_arr, M,Lc,mc,rng, chunk, temp_start=0.2, bal_start=0.3, bal_end=0.6, step_start=0.1)
      update
   return best
```

`_upsample_coarse_to_full(coarse, parent, n)`:
```
def _upsample_coarse_to_full(coarse, parent, n):
   M=len(coarse)
   coarse=_normalize(coarse)
   starts=np.floor(np.arange(M+1)*n/M).astype(int)
   masses=np.zeros(n)
   for i in range(M):
      s=starts[i]; e=starts[i+1]
      if e>s: masses[s:e]=coarse[i]/(e-s)
   total=masses.sum()
   if total <=0: masses=np.ones(n)/n
   else: masses /= total
   if parent is not None:
       par=_normalize(parent)
       # add small uniform to avoid zero parent
       par_safe = np.maximum(par, 1e-8*float(np.max(par)) if max>0 else 1e-8)
       a = masses * par_safe
   else: a = masses
   return _normalize(a)
```
But `masses` is mass per full coordinate summing1. Multiplying by par_safe (which sums ~1) then normalize retains coarse block proportions adjusted by parent within block. However if par varies within block, total block mass not exactly coarse[i]/? The normalization across all blocks may alter but okay. Alternative: normalize each block: for each block, set a[start:e] = masses[start:e] * (par_safe[start:e]/sum(par_safe[start:e])). This preserves coarse mass exactly. Let's do that for better.
```
a=zeros(n)
for i: start/end; if e>s:
    win=par_safe[s:e]
    ws=win.sum(); if ws>0: a[s:e]=masses[s:e]*(win/ws)
return normalize(a)
```
Need parent_safe length n; if parent None uniform. This loops over M (≤256), fine.

But `masses` is uniform within block, sum block=coarse[i]; normalizing each window leaves block sum=coarse[i] (since masses sum block * (win/win sum) sum = masses sum). Good. If parent has zeros, win safe floor.

If levels coarse to coarse (upsample 64 to128), parent can be previous coarse? We can use parent=coarsen(full_parent, N)? Or just uniform. Simpler use parent=None in hierarchy. Let's write `_lp_start` with guard:
```
def _lp_start(base, n,L,m, base_score, rng):
   if linprog is None: return base
   M=128
   f=_coarsen(base,M)
   d=_lp_coarse_direction(f,M)
   if d is None: return base
   full=_upsample_coarse_to_full(d, base, n)
   best_a=base; best_sc=base_score
   for alpha in (0.15,0.3,0.5,0.8,1.0):
       a2=_normalize((1-alpha)*base+alpha*full)
       sc=_score_a(a2,n,L,m)
       if sc < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=a2
   if best_sc > base_score - 1e-12:
       # still return a mildly mixed direction for optimizer to explore
       return _normalize(0.75*base + 0.25*full)
   return best_a
```
If base_score inf? no.

Let's write `_next_pow2`:
```
def _next_pow2(x):
    x=int(max(1,x))
    r=1
    while r < x:
        r <<= 1
    return r
```

`_coarsen`:
```
def _coarsen(a, M):
    a=_normalize(a)
    n=len(a)
    if M <=0: return None
    if M >= n:
        # downsample by averages of max(1,n/M)? but M<n in use
        if M == n: return a.copy()
        # truncate or repeat? For safety, interpolate? We'll handle.
    cm = np.cumsum(a)
    cm = np.concatenate(([0.0], cm))
    fine_edges = np.arange(n+1, dtype=float) * (float(M)/float(n))
    coarse_edges = np.interp(np.arange(M+1, dtype=int), fine_edges, cm, left=0.0, right=cm[-1])
    coarse = np.diff(coarse_edges)
    return _normalize(coarse)
```
Works if M<n. If M>=n, use upsample? For coarse_search M=128/256 <6121. Good. If parent n smaller than M? parent length chosen >=2000. Good.

`_upsample_coarse_to_full` with parent array maybe None:
```
def _upsample_coarse_to_full(coarse, parent, n):
    M=len(coarse)
    coarse=_normalize(coarse)
    sectors=np.floor(np.arange(M+1, dtype=int)*float(n)/float(M)).astype(int)
    # ensure monotonic
    masses=np.zeros(n)
    for i in range(M):
       s=sectors[i]; e=sectors[i+1]
       if e>s: masses[s:e]=coarse[i]/(e-s)
    total = masses.sum()
    if total <= 0: masses=np.ones(n)/n
    else: masses/=total
    if parent is None:
       return _normalize(masses)
    par = np.asarray(parent, float).ravel()
    if par.size != n:
       # try coarsen? If parent length not n, use masses
       pass
    else:
       par = np.where(np.isfinite(par), par, 0.0)
       par = np.maximum(par,0.0)
       maxpar=float(np.max(par)) if par.size else 0.0
       floor = 0.0
       if maxpar >0: floor=1e-8*maxpar
       par = np.maximum(par, floor)
       sw=par.sum()
       if sw <=0: par=np.ones(n)/n
       else: par=par/sw
       a=np.zeros(n)
       for i in range(M):
          s=sectors[i]; e=sectors[i+1]
          if e>s:
             win=par[s:e]
             wsz=float(np.sum(win))
             if wsz >0:
                a[s:e] = masses[s:e] * (win / wsz)
       return _normalize(a)
    return _normalize(masses)
```
Note if maxpar=0, floor=0, par sum0 -> set uniform. Good.

Potential inefficiency: sectors may have zero length sectors if M>n? Not; M<n. Some sectors length floor/ceil. Good.

`_noise_mix`:
```
def _noise_mix(a,rng,alpha):
   a=_normalize(a)
   prior=_random_prior(a.size,rng)
   amax=float(np.max(a))
   floor = 1e-8*amax if amax>0 else 1e-8
   a_safe=np.maximum(a,floor)
   pmax=float(np.max(prior)); pfloor=1e-8*pmax if pmax>0 else 1e-8
   p_safe=np.maximum(prior,pfloor)
   loga=np.log(a_safe); logp=np.log(p_safe)
   z = (1-alpha)*loga + alpha*logp
   z -= z.max()
   a2=np.exp(z)
   a2=np.maximum(a2, 1e-300)
   return _normalize(a2)
```
If alpha=0? returns random? Alpha in [0.1,1]. Good.

`_harsh_mix`:
```
def _harsh_mix(a,rng):
   a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
   keep = 0.4 + 0.35*float(rng.random())
   thresh = np.quantile(a, 1.0 - keep)
   mask = a >= thresh
   randmask = rng.random(n) < keep
   mask = mask | randmask
   a2 = a * mask
   if a2.sum() <=0: a2=a.copy()
   return _normalize(a2)
```
This may keep almost all because randmask union. Good.

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a)**2).

This program combines:
  - an FFT-based multiplicative mirror-descent optimizer,
  - a small regularized least-squares "gradient balancing" weight finder,
  - coarse-to-fine global initialization,
  - occasional LP-inspired directions on a coarse grid,
  - MWS-inspired directions,
  - many controlled perturbations and randomized restarts.

The final sequence is checked with the externally provided evaluate_sequence()
if available, and the best exact sequence is returned.
"""

import math
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a[a < 0.0] = 0.0
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a.fill(1.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
    a /= s
    tiny = np.finfo(np.float64).tiny
    a = np.maximum(a, tiny)
    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
        a.fill(1.0 / float(a.size))
    else:
        a /= s2
    return a


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    r = 1
    while r < x:
        r <<= 1
    return r


def _conv_self(a, L, m):
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    F *= F
    c = np.fft.irfft(F, L)[:m]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _score_a(a, n, L, m):
    c = _conv_self(a, L, m)
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def _lse(c, T):
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if T <= 0.0 or cmax <= 0.0:
        return cmax
    z = (c - cmax) / T
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return cmax
    return float(cmax + T * math.log(s))


def _soft_weights(c, T):
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    m = c.size
    if T <= 0.0 or cmax <= 0.0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    z = (c - cmax) / T
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _balanced_weights(c, p, n, lag_count=0, coord_count=0):
    m = c.size
    if m <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, m), dtype=np.float64)

    if lag_count <= 0:
        K = max(8, min(128, int(m / 180.0)))
    else:
        K = int(lag_count)
    K = max(1, min(m, K))

    if coord_count <= 0:
        S = max(24, min(256, int(n / 40.0)))
    else:
        S = int(coord_count)
    S = max(1, min(n, S))

    if K < m:
        idx_k = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    else:
        idx_k = np.arange(m)

    if S < n:
        idx_s = np.argpartition(p, -S)[-S:]
    else:
        idx_s = np.arange(n)

    H = np.zeros((S, K), dtype=np.float64)
    p_local = p
    for j, k in enumerate(idx_k):
        q = k - idx_s
        valid = (q >= 0) & (q < n)
        H[valid, j] = p_local[q[valid]]

    lam = None
    try:
        sol, _, _, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(H, np.ones(S, dtype=np.float64), rcond=None)
        lam = np.maximum(np.asarray(sol, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    except Exception:
        lam = None

    if lam is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(lam)):
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx_k] = 1.0 / float(K)
        return w

    s = float(lam.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx_k] = 1.0 / float(K)
        return w

    lam /= s
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx_k] = lam
    return w


def _cross_corr(w, a, L, m, n):
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], L)
    H = W * Ar
    g = np.fft.irfft(H, L)[(n - 1):(n - 1 + n)]
    g = np.ascontiguousarray(g)
    np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
    return g


def _random_prior(n, rng):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    z = rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, n).astype(np.float64)

    max_k = min(9, max(1, n // 2))
    for k in range(1, max_k + 1):
        amp_c = rng.uniform(0.12, 1.10) / math.sqrt(float(k))
        ph_c = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        z += amp_c * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * k * t + ph_c)

        amp_s = rng.uniform(0.12, 1.10) / math.sqrt(float(k))
        ph_s = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        z += amp_s * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * k * t + ph_s)

    if n > 8:
        z += rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0) * (t - 0.5)
        z += rng.uniform(-0.5, 0.5) * (4.0 * t * (1.0 - t) - 0.5)

    noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.20, n)
    z += noise

    z -= float(np.max(z))
    p = np.exp(z)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    return _normalize(p)


def _noise_mix(a, rng, alpha):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    prior = _random_prior(n, rng)
    alpha = min(1.0, max(0.0, float(alpha)))

    amax = float(np.max(a))
    floor_a = 1e-8 * amax if amax > 0.0 else 1e-8
    pmax = float(np.max(prior))
    floor_p = 1e-8 * pmax if pmax > 0.0 else 1e-8

    a_safe = np.maximum(a, floor_a)
    p_safe = np.maximum(prior, floor_p)

    z = (1.0 - alpha) * np.log(a_safe) + alpha * np.log(p_safe)
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    out = np.exp(z)
    out = np.maximum(out, 1e-300)
    return _normalize(out)


def _harsh_mix(a, rng):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    keep = 0.40 + 0.35 * float(rng.random())
    thr = np.quantile(a, 1.0 - keep)
    mask = a >= thr
    rnd = rng.random(n) < keep
    mask = mask | rnd
    out = a * mask
    if out.sum() <= 1e-300:
        out = a.copy()
    return _normalize(out)


def _coarsen(a, M):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    M = int(max(1, M))
    if M <= 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if M >= n:
        if M == n:
            return a.copy()
        M = n

    cm = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(a)))
    fine_edges = np.arange(n + 1, dtype=np.float64) * (float(M) / float(n))
    coarse_edges = np.interp(np.arange(M + 1, dtype=np.int64), fine_edges, cm,
                             left=0.0, right=cm[-1])
    coarse = np.diff(coarse_edges)
    return _normalize(coarse)


def _upsample_coarse_to_full(coarse, parent, n):
    coarse = _normalize(coarse)
    M = coarse.size
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return coarse.copy()

    sectors = np.floor(np.arange(M + 1, dtype=np.int64) * (float(n) / float(M))).astype(int)
    sectors = np.maximum.accumulate(sectors)
    sectors[0] = 0
    sectors[-1] = n

    masses = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(M):
        s = int(sectors[i])
        e = int(sectors[i + 1])
        if e > s:
            masses[s:e] = coarse[i] / float(e - s)

    total = float(masses.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(total) or total <= 0.0:
        masses = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        masses /= total

    if parent is None:
        return _normalize(masses)

    par = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if par.size != n:
        return _normalize(masses)

    par = np.where(np.isfinite(par), par, 0.0)
    par = np.maximum(par, 0.0)
    par_sum = float(par.sum())
    if par_sum <= 0.0:
        par = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        par /= par_sum

    par_max = float(np.max(par))
    floor = 1e-8 * par_max if par_max > 0.0 else 1e-8
    par = np.maximum(par, floor)

    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(M):
        s = int(sectors[i])
        e = int(sectors[i + 1])
        if e > s:
            win = par[s:e]
            wsz = float(np.sum(win))
            if wsz > 0.0:
                out[s:e] = masses[s:e] * (win / wsz)

    return _normalize(out)


def _build_convolution_matrix(f, M):
    f = _normalize(f)
    M = int(f.size)
    A = np.zeros((2 * M - 1, M), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(2 * M - 1):
        s = max(0, k - (M - 1))
        e = min(k, M - 1)
        if e >= s:
            jv = np.arange(s, e + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            iv = k - jv
            A[k, jv] = f[iv]
    return A


def _lp_coarse_direction(f, M):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    f = _normalize(f)
    M = int(f.size)
    A = _build_convolution_matrix(f, M)
    cvec = -np.ones(M, dtype=np.float64)
    bvec = np.ones(2 * M - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    res = None
    try:
        res = linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=(0.0, 1000.0), method="highs")
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=(0.0, 1000.0), method="interior-point")
        except Exception:
            res = None

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False) or res.x is None:
        return None

    x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    if x.sum() <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return _normalize(x)


def _lp_start(base, n, L, m, base_score, rng):
    if linprog is None:
        return base
    M = 128
    if n < M:
        M = max(32, min(M, n))
    f_c = _coarsen(base, M)
    d = _lp_coarse_direction(f_c, M)
    if d is None:
        return base

    full_dir = _upsample_coarse_to_full(d, base, n)
    best_a = base
    best_sc = float(base_score)

    for alpha in (0.15, 0.30, 0.50, 0.80, 1.0):
        a2 = _normalize((1.0 - alpha) * base + alpha * full_dir)
        sc = _score_a(a2, n, L, m)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a2

    if best_sc > float(base_score) - 1e-12:
        return _normalize(0.75 * base + 0.25 * full_dir)
    return best_a


def _mws_direction(f, L, m, n, prior, inner, eta_start=2.0, eta_end=30.0):
    f = _normalize(f)
    prior = _normalize(prior)
    inner = max(1, int(inner))

    F = np.fft.rfft(f, L)
    Frev = np.fft.rfft(f[::-1], L)

    x = prior.copy()
    y = np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    avg = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    count = 0
    logprior = np.log(np.maximum(prior, 1e-300))

    for it in range(inner):
        frac = 0.0 if inner <= 1 else float(it) / float(inner - 1)
        eta = float(eta_start) + (float(eta_end) - float(eta_start)) * frac

        X = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
        u = np.fft.irfft(F * X, L)[:m]
        np.maximum(u, 0.0, out=u)

        vu = n * u
        vma = float(np.max(vu))
        z = eta * (vu - vma)
        z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
        y = np.exp(z)
        ys = float(y.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ys) or ys <= 0.0:
            y = np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            y /= ys

        avg += x
        count += 1

        Y = np.fft.rfft(y, L)
        s = np.fft.irfft(Y * Frev, L)[(n - 1):(n - 1 + n)]
        np.maximum(s, 0.0, out=s)

        ps = n * s
        sma = float(np.max(ps))
        zx = -eta * (ps - sma) + 0.8 * logprior
        zx -= float(np.max(zx))
        zx = np.clip(zx, -50.0, 0.0)
        x = np.exp(zx)
        xs = float(x.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(xs) or xs <= 0.0:
            x = prior.copy()
        else:
            x /= xs

    if count == 0:
        return prior
    return _normalize(0.5 * (avg / float(count)) + 0.5 * x)


def _mws_start(base, n, L, m, base_score, rng):
    prior = _random_prior(n, rng)
    d = _mws_direction(base, L, m, n, prior, 12, 2.0, 32.0)
    if d is None:
        return base

    best_a = base
    best_sc = float(base_score)
    for alpha in (0.15, 0.30, 0.50, 0.80, 1.0):
        a2 = _normalize((1.0 - alpha) * base + alpha * d)
        sc = _score_a(a2, n, L, m)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a2

    if best_sc > float(base_score) - 1e-12:
        return _normalize(0.70 * base + 0.30 * d)
    return best_a


def _optimize_sequence(a, n, L, m, rng, time_limit,
                       temp_start=0.15, temp_end=0.02,
                       bal_start=0.35, bal_end=0.60,
                       step_start=0.08,
                       bal_lags=0, bal_coords=0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_limit = float(max(0.0, time_limit))
    deadline = t0 + time_limit

    a = _normalize(a)
    if time_limit <= 0.5:
        return a, _score_a(a, n, L, m)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_a(best, n, L, m)
    stale = 0
    no_accept = 0
    it = 0

    temp = float(temp_start)
    eta = float(step_start)
    last_bal = None

    if bal_lags <= 0:
        bal_lags = max(8, min(128, int(m / 180.0)))
    if bal_coords <= 0:
        bal_coords = max(24, min(256, int(n / 40.0)))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 0.30:
            break

        c = _conv_self(a, L, m)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        score = float(2.0 * n * cmax)

        if score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = score
            best = a.copy()
            stale = 0
        else:
            stale += 1

        if best_score <= 1.0005:
            break

        frac = (time.monotonic() - t0) / max(time_limit, 1e-9)
        frac = min(1.0, max(0.0, frac))
        bal_nom = min(0.95, bal_start + (bal_end - bal_start) * frac)
        if stale > 50 or no_accept > 35:
            bal_eff = min(0.20, bal_nom)
            temp_eff = max(temp, 0.45)
        else:
            bal_eff = bal_nom
            temp_eff = temp

        T = max(1e-15, temp_eff * cmax)
        w_soft = _soft_weights(c, T)

        if last_bal is None or (it % 3 == 0):
            last_bal = _balanced_weights(c, a, n, bal_lags, bal_coords)

        w = (1.0 - bal_eff) * w_soft + bal_eff * last_bal
        g = _cross_corr(w, a, L, m, n)
        gmax = float(np.max(g))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 0.0:
            break

        gnorm = g / gmax
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gnorm)):
            break

        cur_lse = _lse(c, T)

        steps = [eta, 0.5 * eta, min(1.0, 1.5 * eta)]
        if eta < 0.04:
            steps.append(0.12)

        accepted = False
        chosen = 0.0

        for step in steps:
            if step <= 1e-8 or step > 1.5:
                continue
            a2 = a * np.exp(-step * gnorm)
            a2 = _normalize(a2)

            c2 = _conv_self(a2, L, m)
            s2 = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c2))

            if s2 < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = s2
                best = a2.copy()

            lse2 = _lse(c2, T)

            if (s2 < score - 1e-13) or (s2 <= best_score + 1e-13):
                a = a2
                accepted = True
                chosen = step
                break

            if lse2 < cur_lse - 1e-11 and s2 <= min(score, best_score) + 1e-7:
                a = a2
                accepted = True
                chosen = step
                break

        if accepted:
            no_accept = 0
            if chosen >= eta - 1e-12:
                eta = min(0.80, eta * 1.10)
            elif chosen > 0.7 * eta:
                eta = min(0.80, eta * 1.03)
            else:
                eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.92)

            if best_score < score - 1e-14:
                temp = max(temp_end, temp * 0.975)
            else:
                temp = max(temp_end, temp * 0.995)
        else:
            no_accept += 1
            stale += 1
            eta = max(0.015, eta * 0.78)
            temp = min(1.60, temp * 1.20)

            if no_accept > 85 or stale > 180:
                alpha = 0.25 + 0.40 * float(rng.random())
                prior = _random_prior(n, rng)
                a = _normalize((1.0 - alpha) * best + alpha * prior)
                temp = max(temp_start * 0.65, temp * 0.55)
                eta = max(0.05, step_start * 0.7)
                no_accept = 0
                stale = 80

        it += 1

    final_score = _score_a(best, n, L, m)
    return best, final_score


def _coarse_search(parent, n_full, rng, time_limit):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    if deadline - t0 <= 5.0:
        return None
    if n_full < 64:
        return None

    M = 256 if time_limit > 180.0 else 128
    if n_full < M:
        M = max(32, min(M, n_full))

    Lc = _next_pow2(2 * M - 1)
    mc = 2 * M - 1

    inits = []
    inits.append(np.ones(M, dtype=np.float64))
    inits.append(_random_prior(M, rng))
    if parent is not None:
        inits.append(_coarsen(parent, M))

    best = None
    best_sc = float("inf")
    for arr in inits:
        if arr is None:
            continue
        arr = _normalize(arr)
        sc = _score_a(arr, M, Lc, mc)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = arr.copy()

    if best is None:
        return None

    # Run 1: polish the current coarse best.
    chunk = min(0.52 * float(time_limit), max(5.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0))
    if chunk > 5.0:
        a2, sc2 = _optimize_sequence(
            best, M, Lc, mc, rng, chunk,
            temp_start=0.20, temp_end=0.02,
            bal_start=0.35, bal_end=0.65,
            step_start=0.10,
            bal_lags=max(8, min(64, int(mc / 180.0))),
            bal_coords=max(16, min(96, int(M / 25.0)))
        )
        if sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best = a2

    # Run 2: noisy perturbation of coarse best.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 15.0:
        chunk = min(0.30 * float(time_limit), max(5.0, rem - 10.0))
        if chunk > 5.0:
            start = _noise_mix(best, rng, 0.30 + 0.25 * float(rng.random()))
            a2, sc2 = _optimize_sequence(
                start, M, Lc, mc, rng, chunk,
                temp_start=0.22, temp_end=0.02,
                bal_start=0.30, bal_end=0.60,
                step_start=0.10,
                bal_lags=max(8, min(64, int(mc / 180.0))),
                bal_coords=max(16, min(96, int(M / 25.0)))
            )
            if sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc2
                best = a2

    # Run 3: fresh random coarse shape.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 15.0:
        chunk = min(0.20 * float(time_limit), max(5.0, rem - 10.0))
        if chunk > 5.0:
            start = _random_prior(M, rng)
            a2, sc2 = _optimize_sequence(
                start, M, Lc, mc, rng, chunk,
                temp_start=0.28, temp_end=0.03,
                bal_start=0.25, bal_end=0.55,
                step_start=0.12,
                bal_lags=max(8, min(64, int(mc / 180.0))),
                bal_coords=max(16, min(96, int(M / 25.0)))
            )
            if sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc2
                best = a2

    return best


def _add_candidate(arrays, scores, arr, score, max_len=12):
    if arr is None:
        return
    try:
        score = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return

    arr = _normalize(arr)

    for s in scores:
        if abs(float(s) - score) <= 1e-10:
            return

    if len(arrays) < max_len:
        arrays.append(arr.copy())
        scores.append(score)
    else:
        worst = max(scores)
        idx = scores.index(worst)
        if score < worst - 1e-12:
            arrays[idx] = arr.copy()
            scores[idx] = score


def _make_sequence(arr, scale=10.0):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    arr = arr / s
    out = arr * float(scale)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    out = np.minimum(out, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in out]


def _internal_exact(seq):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            return float("inf")
        b = np.convolve(a, a)
        mb = float(np.max(b))
        return float(2.0 * a.size * mb / (s * s))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _external_score(seq):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except NameError:
        return _internal_exact(seq)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if val is None:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _parent_length():
    try:
        base = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size > 0 and np.any(np.isfinite(arr)):
            return int(arr.size)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def _get_parent(n):
    try:
        base = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size != n:
            return None
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        if arr.sum() <= 0.0:
            return None
        return _normalize(arr)
    except Exception:
        return None


def _choose_n(total):
    plen = _parent_length()
    if total > 1000.0:
        if plen is not None and 1000 <= plen <= 16000:
            return plen
        return 6121
    if total > 300.0:
        if plen is not None and 512 <= plen <= 12000:
            return min(plen, 6121)
        return 2048
    if plen is not None and plen <= 1024:
        return plen
    return 512


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    """Search for a strong nonnegative coefficient sequence."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(total) or total <= 1.0:
        total = 2400.0

    total = max(10.0, total)
    hard_deadline = start + max(2.0, total - 5.0)

    seed_i = 1000003
    try:
        seed_i = (seed_i + int(seed)) & 0xffffffff
    except Exception:
        pass
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed_i)

    n = _choose_n(total)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    m = 2 * n - 1

    parent = _get_parent(n)
    if parent is None:
        best_a = _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    else:
        best_a = parent

    best_score = _score_a(best_a, n, L, m)

    pool_arrays = []
    pool_scores = []
    _add_candidate(pool_arrays, pool_scores, best_a, best_score, 12)

    print("start n={} L={} internal={:.10f}".format(n, L, best_score), flush=True)

    avail = max(20.0, hard_deadline - start)
    eval_reserve = min(90.0, max(12.0, 0.035 * avail))
    final_polish = min(max(50.0, 0.20 * avail), 0.38 * avail)
    search_deadline = hard_deadline - eval_reserve - final_polish
    if search_deadline <= start + 10.0:
        search_deadline = start + max(5.0, 0.55 * avail)
        final_polish = max(5.0, hard_deadline - eval_reserve - search_deadline)

    # Coarse global initialization.
    coarse_full = None
    if total > 900.0:
        coarse_time = min(240.0, 0.10 * total)
        coarse_time = min(coarse_time, max(0.0, search_deadline - start - 160.0))
        if coarse_time > 25.0:
            t0 = time.monotonic()
            coarse_a = _coarse_search(parent, n, rng, coarse_time)
            if coarse_a is not None:
                coarse_full = _upsample_coarse_to_full(coarse_a, best_a, n)
                coarse_sc = _score_a(coarse_full, n, L, m)
                _add_candidate(pool_arrays, pool_scores, coarse_full, coarse_sc, 12)
                if coarse_sc < best_score:
                    best_score = coarse_sc
                    best_a = coarse_full
                print("coarse {}s coarse_score={:.10f} full_score={:.10f} best={:.10f}".format(
                    time.monotonic() - t0,
                    _score_a(coarse_a, coarse_a.size, _next_pow2(2 * coarse_a.size - 1), 2 * coarse_a.size - 1),
                    coarse_sc, best_score), flush=True)

    # Initial polish of the current best.
    now = time.monotonic()
    initial_time = min(max(60.0, 0.18 * total), max(5.0, search_deadline - now - 40.0))
    if initial_time > 10.0:
        a2, sc2 = _optimize_sequence(
            best_a, n, L, m, rng, initial_time,
            temp_start=0.16, temp_end=0.02,
            bal_start=0.38, bal_end=0.65,
            step_start=0.08,
            bal_lags=max(24, min(160, int(m / 150.0))),
            bal_coords=max(48, min(256, int(n / 35.0)))
        )
        _add_candidate(pool_arrays, pool_scores, a2, sc2, 12)
        if sc2 < best_score:
            best_score = sc2
            best_a = a2
        print("initial polish best={:.10f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    # Polish one alternate candidate, especially the coarse candidate if it is close.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if len(pool_arrays) > 1 and now < search_deadline - 80.0:
        order = list(range(len(pool_arrays)))
        order.sort(key=pool_scores.__getitem__)
        alt_idx = order[1] if len(order) > 1 else order[0]
        alt = pool_arrays[alt_idx]
        alt_sc = pool_scores[alt_idx]
        alt_time = min(160.0, max(20.0, 0.07 * total))
        alt_time = min(alt_time, max(5.0, search_deadline - now - 50.0))
        if alt_time > 15.0 and alt_sc <= best_score + 0.15:
            a2, sc2 = _optimize_sequence(
                alt, n, L, m, rng, alt_time,
                temp_start=0.13, temp_end=0.015,
                bal_start=0.35, bal_end=0.62,
                step_start=0.07,
                bal_lags=max(24, min(160, int(m / 150.0))),
                bal_coords=max(48, min(256, int(n / 35.0)))
            )
            _add_candidate(pool_arrays, pool_scores, a2, sc2, 12)
            if sc2 < best_score:
                best_score = sc2
                best_a = a2
            print("alternate polish best={:.10f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    # Main diversification loop.
    cycle = 0
    while time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 20.0 and cycle < 24:
        rem = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 45.0:
            break

        if rem > 700.0:
            chunk = min(170.0, 0.15 * rem)
        elif rem > 250.0:
            chunk = min(120.0, 0.25 * rem)
        else:
            chunk = max(25.0, rem - 35.0)

        base = best_a
        mode = cycle % 5

        if mode == 0:
            start_a = _noise_mix(base, rng, 0.22 + 0.25 * float(rng.random()))
        elif mode == 1:
            start_a = _harsh_mix(base, rng)
        elif mode == 2:
            if rem > 260.0 and linprog is not None:
                start_a = _lp_start(base, n, L, m, best_score, rng)
            else:
                p = _random_prior(n, rng)
                start_a = _normalize(0.70 * base + 0.30 * p)
        elif mode == 3:
            if rem > 220.0:
                start_a = _mws_start(base, n, L, m, best_score, rng)
            else:
                start_a = _noise_mix(base, rng, 0.55 + 0.20 * float(rng.random()))
        else:
            p = _random_prior(n, rng)
            alpha = 0.55 + 0.30 * float(rng.random())
            start_a = _normalize((1.0 - alpha) * base + alpha * p)

        a2, sc2 = _optimize_sequence(
            start_a, n, L, m, rng, chunk,
            temp_start=0.13, temp_end=0.012,
            bal_start=0.32, bal_end=0.58,
            step_start=0.07,
            bal_lags=max(24, min(160, int(m / 150.0))),
            bal_coords=max(48, min(256, int(n / 35.0)))
        )
        _add_candidate(pool_arrays, pool_scores, a2, sc2, 12)
        if sc2 < best_score:
            best_score = sc2
            best_a = a2
            print("cycle {} improved best={:.10f}".format(cycle, best_score), flush=True)
        else:
            print("cycle {} trial={} best={:.10f}".format(cycle, sc2, best_score), flush=True)

        cycle += 1

        # Keep polishing the incumbent periodically.
        if cycle % 2 == 0 and time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 35.0:
            short = min(70.0, max(15.0, (search_deadline - time.monotonic()) / 3.0))
            if short > 12.0:
                b2, bs2 = _optimize_sequence(
                    best_a, n, L, m, rng, short,
                    temp_start=0.07, temp_end=0.005,
                    bal_start=0.50, bal_end=0.75,
                    step_start=0.04,
                    bal_lags=max(32, min(200, int(m / 120.0))),
                    bal_coords=max(64, min(300, int(n / 30.0)))
                )
                _add_candidate(pool_arrays, pool_scores, b2, bs2, 12)
                if bs2 < best_score:
                    best_score = bs2
                    best_a = b2

    # Final high-intensity polish of the global best.
    now = time.monotonic()
    final_time = min(hard_deadline - now - eval_reserve, final_polish)
    if final_time > 15.0:
        a2, sc2 = _optimize_sequence(
            best_a, n, L, m, rng, final_time,
            temp_start=0.045, temp_end=0.0010,
            bal_start=0.55, bal_end=0.78,
            step_start=0.035,
            bal_lags=max(48, min(260, int(m / 100.0))),
            bal_coords=max(96, min(360, int(n / 25.0)))
        )
        _add_candidate(pool_arrays, pool_scores, a2, sc2, 12)
        if sc2 < best_score:
            best_score = sc2
            best_a = a2
        print("final polish internal={:.10f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    # Final exact evaluation of top internal candidates.
    cand_arrays = [best_a]
    order = list(range(len(pool_arrays)))
    order.sort(key=pool_scores.__getitem__)
    for idx in order:
        if len(cand_arrays) >= 10:
            break
        cand_arrays.append(pool_arrays[idx])

    best_seq = None
    best_exact = float("inf")
    external_ok = None

    for arr in cand_arrays:
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 2.0:
            break
        if arr is None:
            continue

        seq = _make_sequence(arr, 10.0)

        if external_ok is None:
            try:
                val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
                external_ok = True
            except NameError:
                external_ok = False
                val = _internal_exact(seq)
            except Exception:
                external_ok = False
                val = float("inf")
        elif external_ok:
            try:
                val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
                if val is None:
                    val = float("inf")
                val = float(val)
            except Exception:
                val = float("inf")
        else:
            val = _internal_exact(seq)

        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_exact:
            best_exact = val
            best_seq = seq

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = _make_sequence(best_a, 1.0)

    print("done internal={:.10f} exact_best={:.10f}".format(best_score, best_exact), flush=True)
    return best_seq
```
